   162	
   163	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   164	// THE DECLARATION RESOLVER — one model of the engine's own addressing.
   165	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166	
   167	/**
   168	 * Three review rounds in a row found the same shape of defect: the load-time
   169	 * gate kept its OWN model of which declaration positions exist and what a
   170	 * selector reaches, and that replica drifted from the walk it was modelling.
   171	 * Every fix closed one divergence and the next round found the next.
   172	 *
   173	 * So the model is here, once, and BOTH readers go through it: the runtime
   174	 * selector walk (`resolvePath`) descends with `descend`, and the gate
   175	 * resolves every declared reference with the same call. The judge and the
   176	 * executor are the same code — the drift class has nowhere left to live.
   177	 */
   178	
   179	/** One descent the walk can make. These are the only four; every address
   180	 * `evaluateNode` records is a sequence of them. */
   181	export type DeclStep =
   182	  | { readonly kind: "field"; readonly name: string }
   183	  | { readonly kind: "entry" }
   184	  | { readonly kind: "member" }
   185	  | { readonly kind: "keyClass" };
   186	
   187	/** A declaration POSITION: the node the walk evaluates and the address it
   188	 * records for it. They travel together, so a caller cannot hold an address
   189	 * that disagrees with its node. */
   190	export interface DeclCursor {
   191	  readonly node: NodeDecl;
   192	  readonly decl: DeclPath;
   193	}
   194	
   195	/** The address language, written in ONE place. */
   196	export function stepAddress(parent: DeclPath, step: DeclStep): DeclPath {
   197	  switch (step.kind) {
   198	    case "field":
   199	      return `${parent}.${step.name}`;
   200	    case "entry":
   201	      return `${parent}.*`;
   202	    case "member":
   203	      return `${parent}[]`;
   204	    case "keyClass":
   205	      return `${parent}<key>`;
   206	  }
   207	}
   208	
   209	/** A field of a DECLARED map, read as an own property. A declaration's
   210	 * field names are authored text like any other key. */
   211	function ownField(fields: Readonly<Record<string, NodeDecl>>, name: string): NodeDecl | undefined {
   212	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(fields, name) ? fields[name] : undefined;
   213	}
   214	
   215	/** A value-class reference and a union are evaluated AT THEIR REFERRER'S
   216	 * address (`evalValueClassRef` and `evalUnion` dispatch on the same frame),
   217	 * so a step departing from one departs from the node behind it. */
   218	function deref(surface: SurfaceDecl, node: NodeDecl, seen = new Set<string>()): NodeDecl | undefined {
   219	  if (node.kind === "valueClass") {
   220	    if (seen.has(node.valueClass)) return undefined;
   221	    seen.add(node.valueClass);
   222	    const target = surface.valueClasses[node.valueClass];
   223	    return target === undefined ? undefined : deref(surface, target, seen);
   224	  }
   225	  if (node.kind === "union") {
   226	    return node.mapCase === undefined ? undefined : deref(surface, node.mapCase, seen);
   227	  }
   228	  return node;
   229	}
   230	
   231	function childDecl(node: NodeDecl, step: DeclStep): NodeDecl | undefined {
   232	  switch (step.kind) {
   233	    case "field":
   234	      return node.kind === "map.fixed" ? ownField(node.fields, step.name) : undefined;
   235	    case "entry":
   236	      return node.kind === "map.open" ? node.entry : undefined;
   237	    case "member":
   238	      return node.kind === "list" ? node.member : undefined;
   239	    case "keyClass":
   240	      return node.kind === "map.open" ? node.keyClass : undefined;
   241	  }
   242	}
   243	
   244	/** The node behind a position, after the reference and union hops the walk
   245	 * takes transparently. Undefined where the reference does not resolve. */
   246	export function derefNode(surface: SurfaceDecl, node: NodeDecl): NodeDecl | undefined {
   247	  return deref(surface, node);
   248	}
   249	
   250	/** THE DESCENT: from position `at`, where a `step` lands — or undefined
   251	 * where the walk does not descend there at all. */
   252	export function descend(surface: SurfaceDecl, at: DeclCursor, step: DeclStep): DeclCursor | undefined {
   253	  const node = deref(surface, at.node);
   254	  if (node === undefined) return undefined;
   255	  const child = childDecl(node, step);
   256	  return child === undefined ? undefined : { node: child, decl: stepAddress(at.decl, step) };
   257	}
   258	
   259	/** A SELECTOR segment as a descent: `*` is the open-map wildcard the
   260	 * language reserves, anything else names a declared field. A literal key
   261	 * at an open map is a DOCUMENT key, not a declared position — the walk
   262	 * records no address for it, so no rule can be hung there. */
   263	export function selectorStep(segment: string): DeclStep {
   264	  return segment === "*" ? { kind: "entry" } : { kind: "field", name: segment };
   265	}
   266	
   267	/** Where a selector path lands, resolved against the DECLARATION alone —
   268	 * the dry run of `resolvePath`. `visited` runs base-first, target last. */
   269	export interface DeclResolution {
   270	  readonly visited: readonly DeclCursor[];
   271	  /** The address the walk failed to reach, when it did. */
   272	  readonly unreached?: string;
   273	}
   274	
   275	export function resolveSelectorPath(
   276	  surface: SurfaceDecl,
   277	  root: DeclCursor,
   278	  parent: DeclCursor | undefined,
   279	  path: string,
   280	): DeclResolution {
   281	  const segments = path.split(".");
   282	  const head = segments.shift();
   283	  const base = head === "$" ? root : parent;
   284	  // The address the path INTENDS, for the message: a reader needs the whole
   285	  // thing, not the segment the walk happened to stop on.
   286	  const intended = base === undefined ? path : [base.decl, ...segments].join(".");
   287	  if (base === undefined) return { visited: [], unreached: intended };
   288	  const visited: DeclCursor[] = [base];
   289	  let cursor = base;
   290	  for (const segment of segments) {
   291	    const next = descend(surface, cursor, selectorStep(segment));
   292	    if (next === undefined) return { visited, unreached: intended };
   293	    cursor = next;
   294	    visited.push(cursor);
   295	  }
   296	  return { visited };
   297	}
   298	
   299	/**
   300	 * The RUNTIME shapes a declared node can legally hold. A selector reads the
   301	 * RAW value, so what decides whether a relation can read a target is not
   302	 * the node's kind name but the shapes a valid value can take — and a node
   303	 * that can hold more than one is readable only for SOME inputs, which is
   304	 * the input-dependent silence a closed declaration must not contain.
   305	 */
   306	export type RuntimeShape = "map" | "list" | "string" | "other";
   307	
   308	export function shapesOf(surface: SurfaceDecl, node: NodeDecl, seen = new Set<string>()): ReadonlySet<RuntimeShape> {
   309	  switch (node.kind) {
   310	    case "map.fixed":
   311	    case "map.open":
   312	    case "map.plain":
   313	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["map"]);
   314	    case "list":
   315	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["list"]);
   316	    case "string":
   317	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["string"]);
   318	    case "integer":
   319	    case "delegate":
   320	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["other"]);
   321	    case "enum":
   322	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(node.members.map((member) => (typeof member.value === "string" ? "string" : "other")));
   323	    case "union": {
   324	      const shapes = new Set<RuntimeShape>((node.literals ?? []).map(() => "string" as const));
   325	      if (node.mapCase !== undefined) for (const shape of shapesOf(surface, node.mapCase, seen)) shapes.add(shape);
   326	      return shapes;
   327	    }
   328	    case "valueClass": {
   329	      if (seen.has(node.valueClass)) return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["other"]);
   330	      seen.add(node.valueClass);
   331	      const target = surface.valueClasses[node.valueClass];
   332	      return target === undefined ? new Set<RuntimeShape>(["other"]) : shapesOf(surface, target, seen);
   333	    }
   334	    case "raw":
   335	      // Uninterpreted: the declaration guarantees nothing about the value.
   336	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["map", "list", "string", "other"]);
   337	  }
   338	}
   339	
   340	/** What each selector relation READS, as one definition: the predicate
   341	 * `evaluateSelector` applies at run time and the shape the gate demands of
   342	 * every legal value. Changing what a relation reads changes both. */
   343	export const RELATION_READS = {
   344	  keysOf: { shape: "map", test: (value: unknown): boolean => isContainer(value) },
   345	  valuesOf: { shape: "list", test: (value: unknown): boolean => Array.isArray(value) },
   346	  collect: { shape: "string", test: (value: unknown): boolean => typeof value === "string" },
   347	} as const satisfies Readonly<Record<string, { readonly shape: RuntimeShape; readonly test: (value: unknown) => boolean }>>;
   348	
   349	export type SelectorRelation = keyof typeof RELATION_READS;
   350	
   351	/** Which relation a selector leaf names, and the path it reads. */
   352	export function selectorRead(
   353	  selector: Extract<Selector, { keysOf: string } | { valuesOf: string } | { collect: string }>,
   354	): { readonly relation: SelectorRelation; readonly path: string } {
   355	  if ("keysOf" in selector) return { relation: "keysOf", path: selector.keysOf };
   356	  if ("valuesOf" in selector) return { relation: "valuesOf", path: selector.valuesOf };
   357	  return { relation: "collect", path: selector.collect };
   358	}
   359	
   360	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   361	// Paths and message interpolation.
   362	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   363	
   364	/** `$` is the template root; `""` is a delegated config's own root. Both
   365	 * are addressed by their children's bare key (the measured path grammar). */
   366	function joinPath(parent: string, key: string): string {
   367	  return parent === "$" || parent === "" ? key : `${parent}.${key}`;
   368	}
   369	
   370	function indexPath(parent: string, index: number): string {
   450	  readonly at: DeclCursor;
   451	  /** The position of the enclosing container — the declaration operand of a
   452	   * `^` selector, exactly as `parentValue` is its value operand. It is NOT
   453	   * derived by chopping `at.decl`: a list member's address ends in `[]`,
   454	   * which chopping resolves to the wrong container. */
   455	  readonly parentAt?: DeclCursor | undefined;
   456	  /** The value path the findings address (`steps.review.role`). */
   457	  readonly path: string;
   458	  /** The SOURCE-document address, carried segment by segment as the walk
   459	   * descends. It is not derived from `path`: a rendered path is lossy —
   460	   * an authored id may itself contain `[0]` or a dot, and re-parsing one
   461	   * addresses a different node or none (B1 F3). */
   462	  readonly segments: readonly (string | number)[];
   463	  readonly value: unknown;
   464	  /** The enclosing container's value — the operand of a `..` selector. */
   465	  readonly parentValue: unknown;
   466	  /** The key of the nearest enclosing OPEN-map entry. */
   467	  readonly ownerKey?: string | undefined;
   468	  readonly label?: string | undefined;
   469	}
   470	
   471	/** What a node evaluation reports upward: whether the node produced a
   472	 * value its dependents may rely on (the implicit container precondition
   473	 * plus every DECLARED `gating`). */
   474	interface NodeResult {
   475	  readonly ok: boolean;
   476	  readonly value?: unknown;
   477	}
   478	
   479	/** A membership lane whose operand has NOT been evaluated yet. The engine
   480	 * defers it to the end of the walk rather than reading an operand whose
   481	 * reliability is still unknown — a GENERAL property of the `memberOf`
   482	 * construct, not a per-rule placement. */
   483	interface DeferredCheck {
   484	  readonly frame: Frame;
   485	  readonly rule: MembershipRule;
   486	  readonly candidate: unknown;
   487	  readonly slots: Slots;
   488	}
   489	
   490	class Run {
   491	  readonly findings: ValidationFinding[] = [];
   492	  readonly effectiveConfigs = new Map<string, unknown>();
   493	  readonly runtimeContextBindings: string[] = [];
   494	  /** Declaration path / tag → whether every instance produced a usable
   495	   * value. A rule naming an unreliable operand is suppressed. */
   496	  readonly declOk = new Map<string, boolean>();
   497	  /** Declaration paths whose evaluation has completed. An operand that is
   498	   * neither reliable nor unreliable but PENDING defers its rule. */
   499	  readonly completed = new Set<DeclPath>();
   500	  readonly deferred: DeferredCheck[] = [];
   501	  readonly memo = new WeakMap<object, unknown>();
   502	  root: unknown;
   503	
   504	  constructor(
   505	    readonly surface: SurfaceDecl,
   506	    readonly channel: EngineChannel,
   507	    readonly registries: Readonly<Record<string, GateCatalog | undefined>>,
   508	  ) {}
   509	
   510	  get valueClasses(): Readonly<Record<string, NodeDecl>> {
   511	    return this.surface.valueClasses;
   512	  }
   513	
   514	  /** The document root as a declaration position — the operand of every
   515	   * `$`-rooted selector. */
   516	  get rootAt(): DeclCursor {
   517	    return { node: this.surface.root, decl: "$" };
   518	  }
   519	
   520	  /** The injected set a selector root or a delegation names. */
   521	  registry(name: string): GateCatalog | undefined {
   522	    return this.registries[name];
   523	  }
   524	
   525	  emit(path: string, message: string, code?: string): void {
   526	    this.findings.push(code === undefined ? { path, message } : { path, message, code });
   527	  }
   528	
   529	  mark(key: string, ok: boolean): void {
   530	    if (this.declOk.get(key) === false) return;
   531	    this.declOk.set(key, ok);
   532	  }
   533	
   534	  reliable(key: string): boolean {
   535	    return this.declOk.get(key) !== false;
   536	  }
   537	}
   538	
   539	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   540	// Selectors.
   541	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   542	
   543	type SelectorResult =
   544	  | { readonly status: "ok"; readonly values: readonly string[] }
   545	  | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" };
   546	
   547	const UNRELIABLE = { status: "unreliable" } as const;
   548	const PENDING = { status: "pending" } as const;
   549	
   550	/** Walk a selector path over the value graph, expanding `*` over open maps.
   551	 * `^` resolves to the citing node's own container. A prefix that has not
   552	 * been evaluated yet answers PENDING, not "unreliable".
   553	 *
   554	 * The DECLARATION side of this walk goes through `descend` — the same call
   555	 * the load-time gate makes — so a path the gate resolved is a path this
   556	 * walk reaches, by construction rather than by two implementations
   557	 * agreeing. */
   558	function resolvePath(
   559	  run: Run,
   560	  from: Frame,
   561	  path: DeclPath,
   562	): { readonly status: "ok"; readonly nodes: unknown[] } | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" } {
   563	  const segments = path.split(".");
   564	  let nodes: unknown[];
   565	  let cursor: DeclCursor | undefined;
   566	  const head = segments.shift();
   567	  if (head === "$") {
   568	    nodes = [run.root];
   569	    cursor = run.rootAt;
   570	  } else if (head === "^") {
   571	    // Parent-relative: the citing node's own container.
   572	    nodes = [from.parentValue];
   573	    cursor = from.parentAt;
   574	  } else {
   575	    return UNRELIABLE;
   576	  }
   577	  if (cursor === undefined) return UNRELIABLE;
   578	  for (const segment of segments) {
   579	    const next = descend(run.surface, cursor, selectorStep(segment));
   580	    if (next === undefined) return UNRELIABLE;
   581	    cursor = next;
   582	    if (!run.reliable(cursor.decl)) return UNRELIABLE;
   583	    if (!run.completed.has(cursor.decl)) return PENDING;
   584	    const values: unknown[] = [];
   585	    for (const node of nodes) {
   586	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   587	      if (segment === "*") {
   588	        for (const [key, child] of entriesOf(node)) {
   589	          if (typeof key === "string") values.push(child);
   590	        }
   591	      } else {
   592	        if (!containerHas(node, segment)) return UNRELIABLE;
   593	        values.push(containerGet(node, segment));
   594	      }
   595	    }
   596	    nodes = values;
   597	  }
   598	  return { status: "ok", nodes };
   599	}
   600	
   601	function evaluateSelector(run: Run, from: Frame, selector: Selector): SelectorResult {
   602	  if ("injected" in selector) {
   603	    // D8's ADMITTED widening: the selector root is an INJECTED set. A
   604	    // registry resolves rather than enumerates, so membership is asked of
   605	    // it directly (`isMember`); the set itself is never listed.
   606	    return run.registry(selector.injected) === undefined ? UNRELIABLE : { status: "ok", values: [] };
   607	  }
   608	  if ("union" in selector) {
   609	    const parts = selector.union.map((part) => evaluateSelector(run, from, part));
   610	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "pending")) return PENDING;
   611	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "unreliable")) return UNRELIABLE;
   612	    return {
   613	      status: "ok",
   614	      values: parts.flatMap((part) => (part.status === "ok" ? part.values : [])),
   615	    };
   616	  }
   617	  const read = selectorRead(selector);
   618	  const resolved = resolvePath(run, from, read.path);
   619	  if (resolved.status !== "ok") return resolved;
   620	  const values: string[] = [];
   621	  for (const node of resolved.nodes) {
   622	    // The SAME predicate the gate demands every legal value satisfy.
   623	    if (!RELATION_READS[read.relation].test(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   624	    if (read.relation === "keysOf") {
   625	      for (const [key] of entriesOf(node as AnyMap)) if (typeof key === "string") values.push(key);
   626	    } else if (read.relation === "valuesOf") {
   627	      for (const item of node as readonly unknown[]) if (typeof item === "string") values.push(item);
   628	    } else {
   629	      values.push(node as string);
   630	    }
   631	  }
   632	  return { status: "ok", values };
   633	}
   634	
   635	/** `memberOf` against an INJECTED root asks the registry; against a
   636	 * document root it asks the collected set. */
   637	function isMember(run: Run, selector: Selector, set: SelectorResult, candidate: string): boolean {
   638	  if ("injected" in selector) return run.registry(selector.injected)?.resolve(candidate) != null;
   639	  return set.status === "ok" && set.values.includes(candidate);
   640	}
   641	
   642	function checkMembership(
   643	  run: Run,
   644	  frame: Frame,
   645	  rule: MembershipRule,
   646	  candidate: unknown,
   647	  slots: Slots,
   648	  local?: ReadonlyMap<string, boolean>,
   649	): void {
   650	  // Suppression is the CITING INSTANCE's business. Where a sibling status
   651	  // exists it decides; the global tag status is sticky across every
   652	  // instance of a declaration path, so consulting it first let one
   653	  // open-map entry's failure suppress a different, valid entry's finding
   654	  // — and only when the broken one came first in the document.
   655	  const suppressed = rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) =>
   656	    local?.has(tag) === true ? local.get(tag) === false : !run.reliable(tag),
   657	  );
   658	  if (suppressed === true) return;
   659	  const set = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.target);
   660	  if (set.status === "pending") {
   661	    // The decision is taken NOW, while the siblings are in scope: by the
   662	    // time the queue drains they are gone.
   663	    run.deferred.push({ frame, rule, candidate, slots });
   664	    return;
   665	  }
   666	  if (set.status === "unreliable") return;
   667	  const at = rule.at === "container" ? parentPath(frame.path) : frame.path;
   668	  const hit = typeof candidate === "string" && isMember(run, rule.target, set, candidate);
   669	  const violated = rule.relation === "disjointFrom" ? hit : !hit;
   670	  if (violated) {
   671	    run.emit(at, render(rule.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: candidate }), rule.code);
   672	  }
   673	}
   674	
   675	function parentPath(path: string): string {
   676	  const bracket = path.lastIndexOf("[");
   677	  const dot = path.lastIndexOf(".");
   678	  if (bracket > dot) return path.slice(0, bracket);
   679	  if (dot < 0) return "$";
   680	  return path.slice(0, dot);
   750	    }
   751	    return undefined;
   752	  } finally {
   753	    ancestors.delete(value);
   754	  }
   755	}
   756	
   757	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   758	// Node evaluation.
   759	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   760	
   761	const DEFAULT_MISSING = 'missing required key "{key}"';
   762	
   763	/** Sibling reliability inside ONE fixed-map evaluation. `dependsOn` reads
   764	 * it before the global map so a defect in binding #1 cannot suppress a
   765	 * lane in binding #2. */
   766	type LocalOk = Map<string, boolean>;
   767	
   768	function baseSlots(frame: Frame): Slots {
   769	  return { path: frame.path, value: frame.value, ownerKey: frame.ownerKey, label: frame.label };
   770	}
   771	
   772	function evaluateNode(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   773	  const result = dispatch(run, decl, frame, local);
   774	  run.completed.add(frame.at.decl);
   775	  run.mark(frame.at.decl, result.ok);
   776	  run.mark(decl.tag, result.ok);
   777	  return result;
   778	}
   779	
   780	function dispatch(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   781	  switch (decl.kind) {
   782	    case "map.fixed":
   783	      return evalMapFixed(run, decl, frame);
   784	    case "map.open":
   785	      return evalMapOpen(run, decl, frame);
   786	    case "map.plain":
   787	      return evalMapPlain(run, decl, frame);
   788	    case "list":
   789	      return evalList(run, decl, frame, local);
   790	    case "string":
   791	      return evalString(run, decl, frame, local);
   792	    case "integer":
   793	      return evalInteger(run, decl, frame);
   794	    case "enum":
   795	      return evalEnum(run, decl, frame);
   796	    case "union":
   797	      return evalUnion(run, decl, frame);
   798	    case "raw":
   799	      return evalRaw(run, decl, frame);
   800	    case "valueClass":
   801	      return evalValueClassRef(run, decl, frame, local);
   802	    case "delegate":
   803	      return evalDelegate(run, decl, frame, local);
   804	  }
   805	}
   806	
   807	function evalValueClassRef(
   808	  run: Run,
   809	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "valueClass" }>,
   810	  frame: Frame,
   811	  local?: LocalOk,
   812	): NodeResult {
   813	  const target = run.valueClasses[decl.valueClass];
   814	  if (target === undefined) return { ok: true, value: frame.value };
   815	  const merged: Frame = { ...frame, label: decl.label ?? frame.label };
   816	  const result = dispatch(run, target, merged, local);
   817	  // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   818	  if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(frame.at.decl, false);
   819	  return result;
   820	}
   821	
   822	function evalMapFixed(
   823	  run: Run,
   824	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.fixed" }>,
   825	  frame: Frame,
   826	): NodeResult {
   827	  const value = frame.value;
   828	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   829	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   830	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   831	    return { ok: false };
   832	  }
   833	  const container = value;
   834	  // Every field position resolved ONCE, through the engine's own descent —
   835	  // the same call the gate makes to resolve a reference TO one of them.
   836	  const fields = new Map<string, DeclCursor>();
   837	  for (const name of Object.keys(decl.fields)) {
   838	    const at = descend(run.surface, frame.at, { kind: "field", name });
   839	    if (at !== undefined) fields.set(name, at);
   840	  }
   841	  const fieldNames = [...fields.keys()];
   842	  // A field declared for ONE channel is legal only there — the same
   843	  // channel scoping D1 applies to source-bearing attributes, applied to
   844	  // keyset membership.
   845	  const inChannel = (name: string): boolean => {
   846	    const channel = fields.get(name)?.node.channel;
   847	    return channel === undefined || channel === "both" || channel === run.channel.kind;
   848	  };
   849	  // A channel-scoped field is scoped WHOLE: out of channel it is neither a
   850	  // legal key, nor a missing one, nor evaluated (design review F7).
   851	  const scoped = fieldNames.filter(inChannel);
   852	  const legal = new Set(scoped);
   853	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   854	  const local: LocalOk = new Map();
   855	
   856	  const emitUnknownKeys = (): void => {
   857	    for (const [key] of entriesOf(container)) {
   858	      if (typeof key !== "string") {
   859	        run.emit(
   860	          frame.path,
   861	          render(decl.unknownMessage, { ...slots, key, value: key, keys: scoped.join(", ") }),
   862	        );
   863	        continue;
   864	      }
   865	      if (legal.has(key)) continue;
   866	      const at = joinPath(frame.path, key);
   867	      const removed = ownMessage(decl.removedKeys, key);
   868	      const keySlots: Slots = { ...slots, path: at, key, value: key, keys: scoped.join(", ") };
   869	      run.emit(at, render(removed ?? decl.unknownMessage, keySlots));
   870	    }
   871	  };
   872	
   873	  const emitMissing = (name: string): void => {
   874	    const fieldAt = fields.get(name);
   875	    const field = fieldAt?.node;
   876	    const presence = field?.presence;
   877	    if (fieldAt === undefined || field === undefined || presence?.required !== true) return;
   878	    if (containerHas(container, name)) return;
   879	    if (presence.foldedIntoTypeLane === true) {
   880	      // Absence is this node's OWN type lane (a binding's `uses`).
   881	      evaluateNode(run, field, childFrame(frame, fieldAt, name, undefined, container), local);
   882	      local.set(field.tag, false);
   883	      return;
   884	    }
   885	    const at = presence.at === "self" ? joinPath(frame.path, name) : frame.path;
   886	    run.emit(
   887	      at,
   888	      render(presence.message ?? decl.missingMessage ?? DEFAULT_MISSING, { ...slots, path: at, key: name }),
   889	      presence.code,
   890	    );
   891	    local.set(field.tag, false);
   892	  };
   893	
   894	  const evalOneField = (name: string): void => {
   895	    const at = fields.get(name);
   896	    if (at === undefined) return;
   897	    const field = at.node;
   898	    if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   899	      // An ABSENT field is where a declared `default:` materializes — the
   900	      // schema-side half of ADR-019 D3.
   901	      if (field.default !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, field.default);
   902	      return;
   903	    }
   904	    const child = childFrame(frame, at, name, containerGet(container, name), container);
   905	    const result = evaluateNode(run, field, child, local);
   906	    local.set(field.tag, result.ok);
   907	    if (result.ok && result.value !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, result.value);
   908	  };
   909	
   910	  const order = decl.laneOrder ?? "unknownThenPerKey";
   911	  if (order === "missingThenUnknown") {
   912	    for (const name of scoped) emitMissing(name);
   913	    emitUnknownKeys();
   914	    for (const name of scoped) evalOneField(name);
   915	  } else if (order === "unknownThenMissingThenValues") {
   916	    emitUnknownKeys();
   917	    for (const name of scoped) emitMissing(name);
   918	    for (const name of scoped) evalOneField(name);
   919	  } else {
   920	    emitUnknownKeys();
   921	    for (const name of scoped) {
   922	      if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   923	        emitMissing(name);
   924	        evalOneField(name);
   925	        continue;
   926	      }
   927	      evalOneField(name);
   928	    }
   929	  }
   930	  return { ok: true, value: normalized };
   931	}
   932	
   933	function childFrame(parent: Frame, at: DeclCursor, key: string, value: unknown, parentValue: unknown): Frame {
   934	  return {
   935	    at,
   936	    parentAt: parent.at,
   937	    path: joinPath(parent.path, key),
   938	    segments: [...parent.segments, key],
   939	    value,
   940	    parentValue,
   941	    ownerKey: parent.ownerKey,
   942	  };
   943	}
   944	
   945	function evalMapOpen(
   946	  run: Run,
   947	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.open" }>,
   948	  frame: Frame,
   949	): NodeResult {
   950	  const value = frame.value;
   951	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   952	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   953	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   954	    return { ok: false };
   955	  }
   956	  const container = value;
   957	  const entries = entriesOf(container);
   958	  let ok = true;
   959	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && entries.length === 0) {
   960	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   961	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   962	  }
   963	  if (decl.deepKeyStringness?.channel === run.channel.kind) {
   964	    scanKeyStringness(run, container, frame.path, decl.deepKeyStringness.message, new WeakSet());
   965	  }
   966	  const keyAt = descend(run.surface, frame.at, { kind: "keyClass" });
   967	  const entryAt = descend(run.surface, frame.at, { kind: "entry" });
   968	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   969	  for (const [key, child] of entries) {
   970	    const keyIsString = typeof key === "string";
   971	    const entryPath = keyIsString ? joinPath(frame.path, key) : frame.path;
   972	    if (decl.keyClass !== undefined && keyAt !== undefined) {
   973	      const keyFrame: Frame = {
   974	        at: keyAt,
   975	        parentAt: frame.at,
   976	        path: decl.keyLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : entryPath,
   977	        segments: frame.segments,
   978	        value: key,
   979	        parentValue: container,
   980	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   981	      };
   982	      const keyResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.keyClass, keyFrame);
   983	      if (!keyResult.ok && decl.keyClass.gating === true) ok = false;
   984	    }
   985	    if (decl.keysSubsetOf !== undefined) {
   986	      const before = run.findings.length;
   987	      // The rule is the OPEN MAP's, so a `^`-relative selector resolves
   988	      // from the map's own container — never from the map itself.
   989	      const subsetFrame: Frame = {
   990	        at: frame.at,
   991	        parentAt: frame.parentAt,
   992	        path: entryPath,
   993	        segments: frame.segments,
   994	        value: key,
   995	        parentValue: frame.parentValue,
   996	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   997	      };
   998	      checkMembership(run, subsetFrame, decl.keysSubsetOf, key, {
   999	        ...slots,
  1000	        path: entryPath,
  1001	        key,
  1002	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
  1003	      });
  1004	      // A key outside the subset is DEAD config: nothing below it has an
  1005	      // operand to be validated against (the measured lane's CONTINUE).
  1006	      if (run.findings.length > before) continue;
  1007	    }
  1008	    if (entryAt === undefined) continue;
  1009	    const entryFrame: Frame = {
  1010	      at: entryAt,
  1011	      parentAt: frame.at,
  1012	      path: entryPath,
  1013	      segments: keyIsString ? [...frame.segments, key] : frame.segments,
  1014	      value: child,
  1015	      parentValue: container,
  1016	      ownerKey: keyIsString ? key : frame.ownerKey,
  1017	    };
  1018	    const entryResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.entry, entryFrame);
  1019	    if (keyIsString && entryResult.ok && entryResult.value !== undefined) {
  1020	      defineOwn(normalized, key, entryResult.value);
  1021	    }
  1022	  }
  1023	  // An open map with NO entries still completes its entry position: a
  1024	  // selector through it must answer "empty", never "not evaluated yet".
  1025	  if (entryAt !== undefined) run.completed.add(entryAt.decl);
  1026	  return { ok, value: normalized };
  1027	}
  1028	
  1029	/** The gates subtree's file-channel key-STRINGNESS scan: a non-string key
  1030	 * cannot become an own property and would blind every own-key scan, so it
  1050	      continue;
  1051	    }
  1052	    scanKeyStringness(run, child, joinPath(path, key), message, seen);
  1053	  }
  1054	}
  1055	
  1056	function evalList(
  1057	  run: Run,
  1058	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "list" }>,
  1059	  frame: Frame,
  1060	  local?: LocalOk,
  1061	): NodeResult {
  1062	  const value = frame.value;
  1063	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
  1064	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
  1065	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
  1066	    return { ok: false };
  1067	  }
  1068	  let ok = true;
  1069	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
  1070	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
  1071	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
  1072	  }
  1073	  const memberAt = descend(run.surface, frame.at, { kind: "member" });
  1074	  if (memberAt === undefined) return { ok, value: [] };
  1075	  const normalized: unknown[] = [];
  1076	  const seen = new Set<unknown>();
  1077	  const clean: { readonly member: unknown; readonly path: string }[] = [];
  1078	  value.forEach((member, index) => {
  1079	    const memberPath = decl.memberLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : indexPath(frame.path, index);
  1080	    const memberFrame: Frame = {
  1081	      at: memberAt,
  1082	      // A list member's `^` operand is the LIST, matching `parentValue`.
  1083	      parentAt: frame.at,
  1084	      path: memberPath,
  1085	      segments: [...frame.segments, index],
  1086	      value: member,
  1087	      parentValue: value,
  1088	      ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
  1089	    };
  1090	    const before = run.findings.length;
  1091	    const result = evaluateNode(run, decl.member, memberFrame, local);
  1092	    if (run.findings.length > before) return;
  1093	    if (result.value !== undefined) normalized.push(result.value);
  1094	    if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
  1095	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.memberOf, member, { ...slots, path: memberPath, value: member });
  1096	    }
  1097	    if (decl.unique?.grain === "perOccurrence") {
  1098	      // `unique.at` is the DUPLICATE lane's own grain; it must not inherit
  1099	      // the member lane's, which `memberLaneAt` sets for a different lane.
  1100	      const at = decl.unique.at === "container" ? frame.path : indexPath(frame.path, index);
  1101	      if (seen.has(member)) {
  1102	        run.emit(at, render(decl.unique.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: member }));
  1103	      }
  1104	      seen.add(member);
  1105	    }
  1106	    clean.push({ member, path: memberPath });
  1107	  });
  1108	  if (decl.disjointFrom !== undefined) {
  1109	    for (const entry of clean) {
  1110	      const memberFrame: Frame = {
  1111	        at: memberAt,
  1112	        parentAt: frame.at,
  1113	        path: entry.path,
  1114	        segments: frame.segments,
  1115	        value: entry.member,
  1116	        parentValue: value,
  1117	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
  1118	      };
  1119	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.disjointFrom, entry.member, {
  1120	        ...slots,
  1121	        path: entry.path,
  1122	        value: entry.member,
  1123	      });
  1124	    }
  1125	  }
  1126	  run.completed.add(memberAt.decl);
  1127	  return { ok, value: normalized };
  1128	}
  1129	
  1130	function evalString(
  1131	  run: Run,
  1132	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "string" }>,
  1133	  frame: Frame,
  1134	  local?: LocalOk,
  1135	): NodeResult {
  1136	  const value = frame.value;
  1137	  const slots: Slots = { ...baseSlots(frame), grammar: decl.grammar?.re };
  1138	  if (typeof value !== "string") {
  1139	    // A node that declares NO type lane leaves the non-string case to its
  1140	    // membership lane, which emits ONE finding for both faults (the
  1141	    // measured form of `start` and of a transition target).
  1142	    const typeLane = decl.typeMessage ?? (decl.memberOf === undefined ? decl.grammar?.message : undefined);
  1143	    if (typeLane !== undefined) {
  1144	      run.emit(frame.path, render(typeLane, slots), decl.presence?.code);
  1145	      return { ok: false };
  1146	    }
  1147	  } else {
  1148	    if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
  1149	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots), decl.presence?.code);
  1150	      return { ok: false };
  1151	    }
  1152	    if (decl.grammar !== undefined && !new RegExp(decl.grammar.re, "u").test(value)) {
  1153	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.grammar.message, slots));
  1154	      return { ok: false };
  1155	    }
  1156	  }
  1157	  if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
  1158	    const before = run.findings.length;
  1159	    checkMembership(run, frame, decl.memberOf, value, slots, local);
  1160	    if (run.findings.length > before) return { ok: false, value };
  1161	  }
  1162	  return typeof value === "string" ? { ok: true, value } : { ok: false };
  1163	}
  1164	
  1165	function evalInteger(
  1166	  run: Run,
  1167	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "integer" }>,
  1168	  frame: Frame,
  1169	): NodeResult {
  1170	  const value = frame.value;
  1171	  if (decl.sourceForm === "plainDecimalInteger" && run.channel.kind === "file") {
  1172	    const node: unknown = run.channel.doc.getIn(frame.segments, true);
  1173	    const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, run.channel.source, frame.path);
  1174	    if (message !== undefined) {
  1175	      run.emit(frame.path, message);
  1176	      return { ok: false };
  1177	    }
  1178	    // The ladder proves the SOURCE form; a declared resolved belt is a
  1179	    // separate obligation and still applies (its own bound may be
  1180	    // stricter than the ladder's built-in one).
  1181	  }
  1182	  const belt = decl.resolvedForm;
  1183	  if (belt?.safeInteger === true) {
  1184	    if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < belt.min) {
  1185	      run.emit(frame.path, render(belt.message, baseSlots(frame)), decl.presence?.code);
  1186	      return { ok: false };
  1187	    }
  1188	  }
  1189	  return { ok: true, value };
  1190	}
  1191	
  1192	function evalEnum(
  1193	  run: Run,
  1194	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "enum" }>,
  1195	  frame: Frame,
  1196	): NodeResult {
  1197	  const value = frame.value;
  1198	  const legal = decl.members.filter(
  1199	    (one) => one.channel === undefined || one.channel === "both" || one.channel === run.channel.kind,
  1200	  );
  1201	  const slots: Slots = {
  1202	    ...baseSlots(frame),
  1203	    members: legal.map((one) => String(one.value)).join(", "),
  1204	  };
  1205	  const member = legal.find((one) => one.value === value);
  1206	  if (member !== undefined) return { ok: true, value: member.store ?? member.value };
  1207	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots), decl.code);
  1208	  return { ok: false };
  1209	}
  1210	
  1211	function evalUnion(
  1212	  run: Run,
  1213	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "union" }>,
  1214	  frame: Frame,
  1215	): NodeResult {
  1216	  const value = frame.value;
  1217	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
  1218	  if (typeof value === "string") {
  1219	    const removed = ownMessage(decl.removedValues, value);
  1220	    if (removed !== undefined) {
  1221	      run.emit(frame.path, render(removed, slots));
  1222	      return { ok: false };
  1223	    }
  1224	    if (decl.literals?.includes(value) === true) return { ok: true, value };
  1225	  }
  1226	  if (isContainer(value) && decl.mapCase !== undefined) {
  1227	    return evaluateNode(run, decl.mapCase, frame);
  1228	  }
  1229	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots));
  1230	  return { ok: false };
  1231	}
  1232	
  1233	function evalRaw(run: Run, decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "raw" }>, frame: Frame): NodeResult {
  1234	  if (decl.containerMessage !== undefined && !isContainer(frame.value)) {
  1235	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, baseSlots(frame)));
  1236	    return { ok: false };
  1237	  }
  1238	  return { ok: true, value: materialize(frame.value, run.memo) };
  1239	}
  1240	
  1241	function evalMapPlain(
  1242	  run: Run,
  1243	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.plain" }>,
  1244	  frame: Frame,
  1245	): NodeResult {
  1246	  const value = frame.value;
  1247	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
  1248	  const realized = materialize(value, run.memo);
  1249	  if (!isPlainMap(realized)) {
  1250	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, { ...slots, value: realized }));
  1251	    return { ok: false };
  1252	  }
  1253	  if (!isCanonicalizable(realized)) {
  1254	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.canonicalJsonSafe.message, slots));
  1255	    return { ok: false };
  1256	  }
  1257	  return { ok: true, value: realized };
  1258	}
  1259	
  1260	function evalDelegate(
  1261	  run: Run,
  1262	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "delegate" }>,
  1263	  frame: Frame,
  1264	  local?: LocalOk,
  1265	): NodeResult {
  1266	  if (decl.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false) === true) return { ok: true };
  1267	  const owner = frame.parentValue;
  1268	  const by = isContainer(owner) ? containerGet(owner, decl.by) : undefined;
  1269	  if (typeof by !== "string") return { ok: true };
  1270	  const registration = run.registry(decl.registry)?.resolve(by) ?? null;
  1271	  if (registration === null) return { ok: true };
  1272	  if (registration.requiresRuntimeContext) run.runtimeContextBindings.push(parentPath(frame.path));
  1273	  const raw = frame.value === undefined ? undefined : materialize(frame.value, run.memo);
  1274	  const result = registration.validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw);
  1275	  if (result.ok) {
  1276	    run.effectiveConfigs.set(parentPath(frame.path), result.effective);
  1277	    return { ok: true, value: result.effective };
  1278	  }
  1279	  if (result.findings.length === 0) {
  1280	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.beltMessage, { ...baseSlots(frame), key: by, value: by }));
  1281	    return { ok: false };
  1282	  }
  1283	  for (const finding of result.findings) {
  1284	    const at = finding.path === "" ? frame.path : `${frame.path}.${finding.path}`;
  1285	    run.emit(at, finding.message, finding.code);
  1286	  }
  1287	  return { ok: false };
  1288	}

  1290	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1291	// Cross rules and the deferred queue.
  1292	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1293	
  1294	function unique(values: readonly string[]): string[] {
  1295	  return [...new Set(values)];
  1296	}
  1297	
  1298	/** vocabulary #12's `equals` — two-direction set equality, each direction
  1299	 * carrying its own path grain and wording (ch8-C16's measured form). */
  1300	function evaluateEquals(run: Run, frame: Frame, rule: EqualsRuleDecl): void {
  1301	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
  1302	  const left = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.left);
  1303	  const right = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.right);
  1304	  if (left.status !== "ok" || right.status !== "ok") return;
  1305	  const declared = unique(left.values);
  1306	  const used = unique(right.values);
  1307	  for (const value of used) {
  1308	    if (declared.includes(value)) continue;
  1309	    const at = render(rule.missingFromLeft.at, { value });
  1310	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromLeft.message, { path: at, value }));
  1311	  }
  1312	  for (const value of declared) {
  1313	    if (used.includes(value)) continue;
  1314	    const at = render(rule.missingFromRight.at, { value });
  1315	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromRight.message, { path: at, value }));
  1316	  }
  1317	}
  1318	
  1319	function runDeferred(run: Run): void {
  1320	  // A membership lane deferred for a PENDING operand runs once, in the
  1321	  // order the walk queued it. The queue is drained, never re-fed: every
  1322	  // operand has completed by now, so a second pass could only loop.
  1323	  const queued = run.deferred.splice(0, run.deferred.length);
  1324	  for (const check of queued) {
  1325	    // `dependsOn` was already decided when this was queued, with the
  1326	    // citing instance's siblings in scope — an empty local map here keeps
  1327	    // the global status from re-deciding it.
  1328	    checkMembership(run, check.frame, check.rule, check.candidate, check.slots, new Map());
  1329	  }
  1330	  run.deferred.length = 0;
  1331	}
  1332	
  1333	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1334	// Entry points.
  1335	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1336	
  1337	/**
  1338	 * Run a surface declaration over a value graph. ONE computation serves
   150	    );
   151	  }
   152	  return problems;
   153	}
   154	
   155	interface Context {
   156	  readonly surface: SurfaceDecl;
   157	  /** The document root as a declaration position — the base of every
   158	   * `$`-rooted path, and the engine's own. */
   159	  readonly root: DeclCursor;
   160	  readonly codes: ReadonlySet<string>;
   161	  readonly tags: string[];
   162	  /** Tags the ENGINE records reliability for — only these can satisfy a
   163	   * `dependsOn` (F4: a substrate or cross-rule tag never gets a status, so
   164	   * depending on one suppresses nothing, forever). */
   165	  readonly nodeTags: Set<string>;
   166	  /** Hook tags whose operand paths are syntactically clean. A path that
   167	   * failed its grammar is NOT resolved a second time — one broken
   168	   * reference must yield one problem, or a maintainer cannot tell how many
   169	   * things are actually wrong. */
   170	  readonly resolvableHooks: Set<string>;
   171	  readonly problems: string[];
   172	}
   173	
   174	/**
   175	 * A position the walk visits on only ONE channel is never evaluated on the
   176	 * other. A rule reading it there waits forever on an operand that never
   177	 * completes, and the deferred queue drops it without a word — the same
   178	 * silence, reached by a different road.
   179	 */
   180	function channelProblem(visited: readonly DeclCursor[], path: string, where: string): string | undefined {
   181	  const scoped = visited.find((cursor) => cursor.node.channel !== undefined && cursor.node.channel !== "both");
   182	  if (scoped === undefined) return undefined;
   183	  return (
   184	    `${where}: selector path ${JSON.stringify(path)} passes through ${JSON.stringify(scoped.decl)}, ` +
   185	    `which is declared channel ${JSON.stringify(scoped.node.channel)} — on the other channel that operand ` +
   186	    `is never evaluated, so the rule waits on it and is dropped in silence`
   187	  );
   188	}
   189	
   190	/** Resolve ONE selector leaf through the engine's own descent, then apply
   191	 * the gate's policy to the answer. */
   192	function leafProblems(
   193	  selector: Extract<Selector, { keysOf: string } | { valuesOf: string } | { collect: string }>,
   194	  where: string,
   195	  ctx: Context,
   196	  base: DeclCursor | undefined,
   197	): string[] {
   198	  const { relation, path } = selectorRead(selector);
   199	  const syntax = selectorPathProblems(path, where);
   200	  if (syntax.length > 0) return syntax;
   201	
   202	  const resolution = resolveSelectorPath(ctx.surface, ctx.root, base, path);
   203	  const target = resolution.visited.at(-1);
   204	  if (resolution.unreached !== undefined || target === undefined) {
   205	    return [
   206	      `${where}: selector path ${JSON.stringify(path)} addresses ` +
   207	        `${JSON.stringify(resolution.unreached ?? path)}, which the declaration does not declare`,
   208	    ];
   209	  }
   210	
   211	  const channel = channelProblem(resolution.visited, path, where);
   212	  if (channel !== undefined) return [channel];
   213	
   214	  // EVERY legal value must be readable. One that is readable for some
   215	  // inputs and silently skipped for others is what "closed" must not mean.
   216	  const needed = RELATION_READS[relation].shape;
   217	  const shapes = shapesOf(ctx.surface, target.node);
   218	  const unreadable = [...shapes].filter((shape) => shape !== needed);
   219	  if (unreadable.length === 0) return [];
   220	  return [
   221	    `${where}: ${relation}(${JSON.stringify(path)}) addresses a ${target.node.kind} whose value can be ` +
   222	      `${[...shapes].sort().join(" | ")}; ${relation} reads only ${needed}, so the rule would be ` +
   223	      `skipped for ${unreadable.sort().join(" | ")} values`,
   224	  ];
   225	}
   226	
   227	function selectorProblems(selector: Selector, where: string, ctx: Context, base: DeclCursor | undefined): string[] {
   228	  if ("injected" in selector) return [];
   229	  if ("union" in selector) return selector.union.flatMap((part) => selectorProblems(part, where, ctx, base));
   230	  return leafProblems(selector, where, ctx, base);
   231	}
   232	
   233	function checkMembership(
   234	  rule: { readonly target: Selector; readonly message: MessageTemplate; readonly code?: string },
   235	  where: string,
   236	  ctx: Context,
   237	  base: DeclCursor | undefined,
   238	): void {
   239	  ctx.problems.push(...selectorProblems(rule.target, where, ctx, base));
   240	  ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(rule.message, where));
   241	  if (rule.code !== undefined && !ctx.codes.has(rule.code)) {
   242	    ctx.problems.push(`${where}: issue code ${JSON.stringify(rule.code)} is outside the declared namespace`);
   243	  }
   244	}
   245	
   246	/** The position a child of `at` occupies, or undefined where the enclosing
   247	 * position is itself unaddressable (inside a value-class DEFINITION, which
   248	 * the engine only ever evaluates at a reference site). */
   249	function childAt(ctx: Context, at: DeclCursor | undefined, step: Parameters<typeof descend>[2]): DeclCursor | undefined {
   250	  return at === undefined ? undefined : descend(ctx.surface, at, step);
   251	}
   252	
   253	/**
   254	 * Walk one node. `channelHonoured` says whether a `channel` mark at THIS
   255	 * position is read by the engine — true only for a field of a `map.fixed`.
   256	 * `at` is the node's own position and `parentAt` its container's — the
   257	 * declaration operands of `$`-relative and `^`-relative paths, carried
   258	 * rather than derived, because a list member's address ends in `[]` and
   259	 * chopping it lands on the wrong container.
   260	 */
   261	function checkNode(
   262	  decl: NodeDecl,
   263	  where: string,
   264	  channelHonoured: boolean,
   265	  ctx: Context,
   266	  at: DeclCursor | undefined,
   267	  parentAt: DeclCursor | undefined,
   268	  owner?: NodeDecl,
   269	): void {
   270	  ctx.tags.push(decl.tag);
   271	  ctx.nodeTags.add(decl.tag);
   272	  if (decl.rows.length === 0) ctx.problems.push(`${where}: node "${decl.tag}" cites no ratified row`);
   273	  if (decl.channel !== undefined && !channelHonoured) {
   274	    ctx.problems.push(
   275	      `${where}: node "${decl.tag}" carries channel ${JSON.stringify(decl.channel)}, ` +
   276	        `which is read only on a field of a map.fixed`,
   277	    );
   278	  }
   279	  if (decl.presence?.code !== undefined && !ctx.codes.has(decl.presence.code)) {
   280	    ctx.problems.push(
   281	      `${where}: issue code ${JSON.stringify(decl.presence.code)} is outside the declared namespace`,
   282	    );
   283	  }
   284	  if (decl.presence?.message !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.presence.message, where));
   285	
   286	  switch (decl.kind) {
   287	    case "map.fixed": {
   288	      ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.containerMessage, where), ...slotProblems(decl.unknownMessage, where));
   289	      if (decl.missingMessage !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.missingMessage, where));
   290	      for (const [name, field] of Object.entries(decl.fields)) {
   291	        checkNode(field, `${where}.${name}`, true, ctx, childAt(ctx, at, { kind: "field", name }), at, decl);
   292	      }
   293	      for (const [key, message] of Object.entries(decl.removedKeys ?? {})) {
   294	        ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(message, `${where}.removedKeys.${key}`));
   295	      }
   296	      return;
   297	    }
   298	    case "map.open": {
   299	      ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.containerMessage, where));
   300	      if (decl.nonempty !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.nonempty.message, where));
   301	      if (decl.deepKeyStringness !== undefined) {
   302	        ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.deepKeyStringness.message, where));
   303	      }
   304	      // The rule is the OPEN MAP's own, so a `^` in it resolves from the
   305	      // map's container — the engine builds its frame that way too.
   306	      if (decl.keysSubsetOf !== undefined) checkMembership(decl.keysSubsetOf, `${where}.keysSubsetOf`, ctx, parentAt);
   307	      if (decl.keyClass !== undefined) {
   308	        checkNode(decl.keyClass, `${where}<key>`, false, ctx, childAt(ctx, at, { kind: "keyClass" }), at);
   309	      }
   310	      checkNode(decl.entry, `${where}.*`, false, ctx, childAt(ctx, at, { kind: "entry" }), at);
   311	      return;
   312	    }
   313	    case "list": {
   314	      ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.containerMessage, where));
   315	      if (decl.nonempty !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.nonempty.message, where));
   316	      if (decl.unique !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.unique.message, where));
   317	      // A member's `^` is the LIST — the engine hands the member frame the
   318	      // list as its `parentValue`, and a list is not a container to read.
   319	      if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) checkMembership(decl.memberOf, `${where}.memberOf`, ctx, at);
   320	      if (decl.disjointFrom !== undefined) checkMembership(decl.disjointFrom, `${where}.disjointFrom`, ctx, at);
   321	      checkNode(decl.member, `${where}[]`, false, ctx, childAt(ctx, at, { kind: "member" }), at);
   322	      return;
   323	    }
   324	    case "string": {
   325	      if (decl.typeMessage !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.typeMessage, where));
   326	      if (decl.nonempty !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.nonempty.message, where));
   327	      if (decl.grammar !== undefined) {
   328	        ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.grammar.message, where));
   329	        try {
   330	          new RegExp(decl.grammar.re, "u");
   331	        } catch {
   332	          ctx.problems.push(`${where}: grammar ${JSON.stringify(decl.grammar.re)} is not a valid regular expression`);
   333	        }
   334	      }
   335	      if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) checkMembership(decl.memberOf, `${where}.memberOf`, ctx, parentAt);
   336	      return;
   337	    }
   338	    case "integer":
   339	      if (decl.resolvedForm !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.resolvedForm.message, where));
   340	      return;
   341	    case "enum": {
   342	      ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.message, where));
   343	      if (decl.code !== undefined && !ctx.codes.has(decl.code)) {
   344	        ctx.problems.push(`${where}: issue code ${JSON.stringify(decl.code)} is outside the declared namespace`);
   345	      }
   346	      return;
   347	    }
   348	    case "union": {
   349	      ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.message, where));
   350	      for (const [value, message] of Object.entries(decl.removedValues ?? {})) {
   351	        ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(message, `${where}.removedValues.${value}`));
   352	      }
   353	      // A union's map case is evaluated AT THE UNION'S OWN ADDRESS.
   354	      if (decl.mapCase !== undefined) checkNode(decl.mapCase, where, false, ctx, at, parentAt);
   355	      return;
   356	    }
   357	    case "raw":
   358	      if (decl.containerMessage !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.containerMessage, where));
   359	      return;
   360	    case "map.plain":
   361	      ctx.problems.push(
   362	        ...slotProblems(decl.containerMessage, where),
   363	        ...slotProblems(decl.canonicalJsonSafe.message, where),
   364	      );
   365	      return;
   366	    case "valueClass": {
   367	      // The measured shame case: an unknown name made the engine return the
   368	      // value unvalidated, so a mistyped class silently switched a rule off.
   369	      if (!(decl.valueClass in ctx.surface.valueClasses)) {
   370	        ctx.problems.push(
   371	          `${where}: value class ${JSON.stringify(decl.valueClass)} is not declared ` +
   372	            `(declared: ${Object.keys(ctx.surface.valueClasses).join(", ")})`,
   373	        );
   374	      }
   375	      return;
   376	    }
   377	    case "delegate": {
   378	      ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.beltMessage, where));
   379	      checkDelegateBy(decl, where, ctx, owner);
   380	      return;
   381	    }
   382	  }
   383	}
   384	
   385	/** The hand-off reads a SIBLING field to name the registration. The engine
   386	 * reads it with `typeof by !== "string" → satisfied`, so a name that
   387	 * resolves to the wrong THING is as silent as one that resolves to
   388	 * nothing: the delegation is skipped and the config never validated. */
   389	function checkDelegateBy(
   390	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "delegate" }>,
   391	  where: string,
   392	  ctx: Context,
   393	  owner: NodeDecl | undefined,
   394	): void {
   395	  const fields = owner === undefined ? undefined : derefNode(ctx.surface, owner);
   396	  const names = fields?.kind === "map.fixed" ? Object.keys(fields.fields) : [];
   397	  const by = fields?.kind === "map.fixed" ? descend(ctx.surface, { node: fields, decl: where }, { kind: "field", name: decl.by }) : undefined;
   398	  if (by === undefined) {
   399	    ctx.problems.push(
   400	      `${where}: delegate reads sibling field ${JSON.stringify(decl.by)}, ` +
   401	        `which the enclosing map does not declare (siblings: ${names.join(", ")})`,
   402	    );
   403	    return;
   404	  }
   405	  if (by.node.channel !== undefined && by.node.channel !== "both") {
   406	    ctx.problems.push(
   407	      `${where}: delegate reads sibling field ${JSON.stringify(decl.by)}, which is declared channel ` +
   408	        `${JSON.stringify(by.node.channel)} — on the other channel it is never present, so the ` +
   409	        `delegation is skipped and the config never validated`,
   410	    );
   411	    return;
   412	  }
   413	  const shapes = shapesOf(ctx.surface, by.node);
   414	  const unreadable = [...shapes].filter((shape) => shape !== "string");
   415	  if (unreadable.length > 0) {
   416	    ctx.problems.push(
   417	      `${where}: delegate reads sibling field ${JSON.stringify(decl.by)}, whose value can be ` +
   418	        `${[...shapes].sort().join(" | ")}; the registration is looked up by a string, so the delegation ` +
   419	        `is skipped for ${unreadable.sort().join(" | ")} values`,
   420	    );
   421	  }
   422	}
   423	
   424	function checkCrossRule(rule: EqualsRuleDecl, ctx: Context): void {
   425	  const where = `crossRule "${rule.tag}"`;
   426	  ctx.tags.push(rule.tag);
   427	  if (rule.rows.length === 0) ctx.problems.push(`${where}: cites no ratified row`);
   428	  // A cross rule cites from the ROOT: it has no citing node, so `^` has no
   429	  // operand and every path it writes must be document-rooted.
   430	  ctx.problems.push(...selectorProblems(rule.left, `${where}.left`, ctx, undefined));
   431	  ctx.problems.push(...selectorProblems(rule.right, `${where}.right`, ctx, undefined));
   432	  ctx.problems.push(...findingPathProblems(rule.missingFromLeft.at, `${where}.missingFromLeft.at`));
   433	  ctx.problems.push(...findingPathProblems(rule.missingFromRight.at, `${where}.missingFromRight.at`));
   434	  ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(rule.missingFromLeft.message, `${where}.missingFromLeft`));
   435	  ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(rule.missingFromRight.message, `${where}.missingFromRight`));
   436	}
   437	
   438	function checkHook(hook: NormalizerHookDecl, ctx: Context): void {
   439	  const where = `normalizer "${hook.tag}"`;
   440	  ctx.tags.push(hook.tag);
   441	  if (hook.rows.length === 0) ctx.problems.push(`${where}: cites no ratified row`);
   442	  const pathProblems = [...normalizerPathProblems(hook.over, `${where}.over`)];
   443	  if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   444	    pathProblems.push(...normalizerPathProblems(hook.advanceSet, `${where}.advanceSet`));
   445	  }
   446	  ctx.problems.push(...pathProblems);
   447	  if (pathProblems.length === 0) ctx.resolvableHooks.add(hook.tag);
   448	}
   449	
   450	/** Where a normalizer operand path lands, through the engine's descent. */
   451	function hookPosition(ctx: Context, path: string): DeclCursor | undefined {
   452	  const resolution = resolveSelectorPath(ctx.surface, ctx.root, undefined, path);
   453	  return resolution.unreached === undefined ? resolution.visited.at(-1) : undefined;
   454	}
   455	
   456	function fieldProblem(ctx: Context, at: DeclCursor, name: string, where: string): string | undefined {
   457	  const node = derefNode(ctx.surface, at.node);
   458	  const names = node?.kind === "map.fixed" ? Object.keys(node.fields) : [];
   459	  if (descend(ctx.surface, at, { kind: "field", name }) !== undefined) return undefined;
   460	  return `${where}: ${JSON.stringify(name)} is not a field of ${JSON.stringify(at.decl)} (fields: ${names.join(", ")})`;
   461	}
   462	
   463	/** Pass two for the normalizer: its operand paths address declared
   464	 * positions, and its dynamic FIELD names are fields of the node it lands
   465	 * on. An unresolved `edges` produced an empty flag map in silence. */
   466	function resolveHooks(surface: SurfaceDecl, ctx: Context): void {
   467	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) {
   468	    if (!ctx.resolvableHooks.has(hook.tag)) continue;
   469	    const where = `normalizer "${hook.tag}"`;
   470	    const over = hookPosition(ctx, hook.over);
   471	    if (over === undefined) {
   472	      ctx.problems.push(`${where}.over: ${JSON.stringify(hook.over)} is not a declared position`);
   473	      continue;
   474	    }
   475	    const entry = descend(ctx.surface, over, { kind: "entry" });
   476	    if (entry === undefined) {
   477	      const kind = derefNode(ctx.surface, over.node)?.kind ?? over.node.kind;
   478	      ctx.problems.push(`${where}.over: ${JSON.stringify(hook.over)} is a ${kind}; the hook walks a map.open`);
   479	      continue;
   480	    }
   481	    if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   482	      if (hookPosition(ctx, hook.advanceSet) === undefined) {
   483	        ctx.problems.push(`${where}.advanceSet: ${JSON.stringify(hook.advanceSet)} is not a declared position`);
   484	      }
   485	      for (const [label, name] of [["edges", hook.edges], ["into", hook.into]] as const) {
   486	        const problem = fieldProblem(ctx, entry, name, `${where}.${label}`);
   487	        if (problem !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(problem);
   488	      }
   489	      continue;
   490	    }
   491	    const member = descend(ctx.surface, entry, { kind: "member" });
   492	    if (member === undefined) {
   493	      ctx.problems.push(`${where}.over: ${JSON.stringify(entry.decl)} is not a list; the hook rebuilds its members`);
   494	      continue;
   495	    }
   496	    for (const name of [...hook.carry, hook.into]) {
   497	      const problem = fieldProblem(ctx, member, name, where);
   498	      if (problem !== undefined) ctx.problems.push(problem);
   499	    }
   500	  }
   501	}
   502	
   503	function checkBranch(
   504	  branch: SubstrateBranch,
   505	  where: string,
   506	  ctx: Context,
   507	  messages: readonly MessageTemplate[] = [],
   508	): void {
   509	  ctx.tags.push(branch.tag);
   510	  if (branch.rows.length === 0) ctx.problems.push(`${where}: cites no ratified row`);
   511	  for (const message of messages) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(message, where));
   512	}
   513	
   514	/** Every `dependsOn` tag any rule names, with the site that named it. */
   515	function collectDependsOn(decl: NodeDecl, where: string, into: { tag: string; where: string }[]): void {
   516	  const add = (tags: readonly string[] | undefined, site: string): void => {
   517	    for (const tag of tags ?? []) into.push({ tag, where: site });
   518	  };
   519	  switch (decl.kind) {
   520	    case "map.fixed":
   520	    case "map.fixed":
   521	      for (const [name, field] of Object.entries(decl.fields)) collectDependsOn(field, `${where}.${name}`, into);
   522	      return;
   523	    case "map.open":
   524	      add(decl.keysSubsetOf?.dependsOn, `${where}.keysSubsetOf`);
   525	      if (decl.keyClass !== undefined) collectDependsOn(decl.keyClass, `${where}<key>`, into);
   526	      collectDependsOn(decl.entry, `${where}.*`, into);
   527	      return;
   528	    case "list":
   529	      add(decl.memberOf?.dependsOn, `${where}.memberOf`);
   530	      add(decl.disjointFrom?.dependsOn, `${where}.disjointFrom`);
   531	      collectDependsOn(decl.member, `${where}[]`, into);
   532	      return;
   533	    case "string":
   534	      add(decl.memberOf?.dependsOn, `${where}.memberOf`);
   535	      return;
   536	    case "union":
   537	      if (decl.mapCase !== undefined) collectDependsOn(decl.mapCase, where, into);
   538	      return;
   539	    case "delegate":
   540	      add(decl.dependsOn, where);
   541	      return;
   542	    default:
   543	      return;
   544	  }
   545	}
   546	
   547	/**
   548	 * Every unresolved reference in a declaration, as a list of problems.
   549	 * Exported so the guard's own fixtures can assert WHICH reference failed
   550	 * rather than only that something did.
   551	 */
   552	export function closureProblems(surface: SurfaceDecl): readonly string[] {
   553	  const ctx: Context = {
   554	    surface,
   555	    root: { node: surface.root, decl: "$" },
   556	    codes: new Set(surface.substrate.codes.values),
   557	    tags: [],
   558	    nodeTags: new Set(),
   559	    resolvableHooks: new Set(),
   560	    problems: [],
   561	  };
   562	
   563	  const substrate = surface.substrate;
   564	  checkBranch(substrate.read, "substrate.read", ctx, [substrate.read.message]);
   565	  checkBranch(substrate.parse.directive, "substrate.parse.directive", ctx, [substrate.parse.directive.message]);
   566	  checkBranch(substrate.parse.duplicateKeys, "substrate.parse.duplicateKeys", ctx, [
   567	    substrate.parse.duplicateKeys.message,
   568	  ]);
   569	  checkBranch(substrate.resolve.graph, "substrate.resolve.graph", ctx, [substrate.resolve.graph.message]);
   570	  checkBranch(substrate.codes, "substrate.codes", ctx);
   571	  checkBranch(substrate.internalFailure, "substrate.internalFailure", ctx, [substrate.internalFailure.message]);
   572	
   573	  checkNode(surface.root, "$", false, ctx, ctx.root, undefined);
   574	  for (const [name, decl] of Object.entries(surface.valueClasses)) {
   575	    // A value-class DEFINITION has no address of its own: the engine only
   576	    // ever evaluates it at a reference site, and its `^` is that site's
   577	    // container. So it is walked without a position, and a `^` written
   578	    // inside one does not resolve — loudly, at load, which is the recorded
   579	    // disposition rather than a silence.
   580	    checkNode(decl, `valueClass "${name}"`, false, ctx, undefined, undefined);
   581	    // A value-class definition's tag is never STATUSED by the engine (the
   582	    // reference's tag is), so it must not count as a dependsOn target.
   583	    ctx.nodeTags.delete(decl.tag);
   584	  }
   585	  for (const rule of surface.crossRules) checkCrossRule(rule, ctx);
   586	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) checkHook(hook, ctx);
   587	
   588	  // Tags must be unique, or a contract pointer is ambiguous.
   589	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   590	  for (const tag of ctx.tags) {
   591	    if (seen.has(tag)) ctx.problems.push(`declaration tag ${JSON.stringify(tag)} is declared more than once`);
   592	    seen.add(tag);
   593	  }
   594	
   595	  // `dependsOn` names a tag the ENGINE gives a reliability status to.
   596	  // Any other declared tag resolves by name and suppresses nothing.
   597	  const dependencies: { tag: string; where: string }[] = [];
   598	  collectDependsOn(surface.root, "$", dependencies);
   599	  for (const [name, decl] of Object.entries(surface.valueClasses)) {
   600	    collectDependsOn(decl, `valueClass "${name}"`, dependencies);
   601	  }
   602	  for (const rule of surface.crossRules) {
   603	    for (const tag of rule.dependsOn ?? []) dependencies.push({ tag, where: `crossRule "${rule.tag}"` });
   604	  }
   605	  for (const dependency of dependencies) {
   606	    if (ctx.nodeTags.has(dependency.tag)) continue;
   607	    ctx.problems.push(
   608	      seen.has(dependency.tag)
   609	        ? `${dependency.where}: dependsOn names ${JSON.stringify(dependency.tag)}, which is declared but is ` +
   610	          `not a node the engine records a status for — depending on it suppresses nothing`
   611	        : `${dependency.where}: dependsOn names ${JSON.stringify(dependency.tag)}, which is not a declared tag`,
   612	    );
   613	  }
   614	
   615	  resolveHooks(surface, ctx);
   616	  return ctx.problems;
   617	}
   618	
   619	function deepFreeze<T>(value: T): T {
   620	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Object.isFrozen(value)) return value;
   621	  Object.freeze(value);
   622	  for (const key of Object.getOwnPropertyNames(value)) {
   623	    deepFreeze((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key]);
   624	  }
   625	  return value;
   626	}
   627	
   628	/**
   629	 * Make a surface declaration usable: CLOSE it, then FREEZE it. A
   630	 * declaration carrying an unresolved reference never becomes a surface —
   631	 * the throw happens at module load, before any document is validated.
   632	 */
   633	export function defineSurface(surface: SurfaceDecl): SurfaceDecl {
   634	  const problems = closureProblems(surface);
   635	  if (problems.length > 0) throw new SurfaceDeclarationError(problems);
   636	  return deepFreeze(surface);
   637	}
     1	import type { NormalizerHookDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "./vocabulary.js";
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * ADR-019 D3: THE NORMALIZER — the engine's SECOND capability, named so
     5	 * that "schema" is never read as covering it.
     6	 *
     7	 * A declaration says what is LEGAL; it does not compute a value. Admission
     8	 * both validates and PRODUCES the admitted form (the audit's residual R4:
     9	 * `advancesRound` expanded per transition, the effective gate config
    10	 * materialized). Those are TRANSFORMS, and they live here.
    11	 *
    12	 * The boundary is exact and load-bearing:
    13	 * - plain `default:` materialization IS declarable and stays SCHEMA-side
    14	 *   (the engine applies it while walking — `activation`, `runtimeContext`,
    15	 *   `output.mode`, the disposition and reason defaults);
    16	 * - DERIVATION — a value computed from other values — is the normalizer's.
    17	 *
    18	 * Each hook is a NAMED capability parameterized by declared operand paths,
    19	 * so adding one is D7 format growth (a ratified amendment), never an edit
    20	 * that slips in under "normalization".
    21	 */
    22	
    23	/** Own-property WRITE: a step id or event type legally admits `__proto__`. */
    24	function defineOwn<T>(target: Record<string, T>, key: string, value: T): void {
    25	  Object.defineProperty(target, key, { configurable: true, enumerable: true, value, writable: true });
    26	}
    27	
    28	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
    29	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    30	}
    31	
    32	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    33	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    34	}
    35	
    36	/** Resolve a declared operand path (`$.steps`, `$.round.advanceOnArrivalAt`)
    37	 * over the normalized value. */
    38	function at(root: unknown, path: string): unknown {
    39	  let cursor: unknown = root;
    40	  for (const segment of path.split(".")) {
    41	    if (segment === "$") continue;
    42	    if (!isRecord(cursor)) return undefined;
    43	    cursor = ownGet(cursor, segment);
    44	  }
    45	  return cursor;
    46	}
    47	
    48	/**
    49	 * R4's first member (ch11-C39): expand each entry's edge map into a
    50	 * COMPLETE per-edge boolean map against the declared advancing set. An
    51	 * absent declaration yields all-false (ch11-C38's none-default); the
    52	 * PRODUCER MONOPOLY holds — the map is recomputed and overwrites whatever
    53	 * the input carried.
    54	 */
    55	function expandFlagMaps(
    56	  root: Record<string, unknown>,
    57	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "expandAdvancesRound" }>,
    58	): void {
    59	  const entries = at(root, hook.over);
    60	  if (!isRecord(entries)) return;
    61	  const declared = at(root, hook.advanceSet);
    62	  const advancing = new Set<string>(Array.isArray(declared) ? (declared as string[]) : []);
    63	  for (const key of Object.keys(entries)) {
    64	    const entry = ownGet(entries, key);
    65	    if (!isRecord(entry)) continue;
    66	    const edges = ownGet(entry, hook.edges);
    67	    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    68	    if (isRecord(edges)) {
    69	      for (const edge of Object.keys(edges)) {
    70	        defineOwn(flags, edge, advancing.has(ownGet(edges, edge) as string));
    71	      }
    72	    }
    73	    defineOwn(entry, hook.into, flags);
    74	  }
    75	}
    76	
    77	/**
    78	 * R4's second member (ch11-C20/C5): write each registration's EFFECTIVE
    79	 * config into the binding's single config surface. The binding is rebuilt
    80	 * from its CARRIED fields plus the effective config — the one downstream
    81	 * config form.
    82	 */
    83	function materializeConfigs(
    84	  root: Record<string, unknown>,
    85	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs" }>,
    86	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
    87	): void {
    88	  // `over` addresses the pipeline map through two open-map levels:
    89	  // `$.steps.*.gates` — the entry key and the event key are the wildcards.
    90	  const [ownerPath, ...rest] = hook.over.split(".*.");
    91	  const owners = at(root, ownerPath ?? "$");
    92	  if (!isRecord(owners)) return;
    93	  const inner = rest.join(".*.");
    94	  for (const ownerKey of Object.keys(owners)) {
    95	    const owner = ownGet(owners, ownerKey);
    96	    if (!isRecord(owner)) continue;
    97	    const pipelines = ownGet(owner, inner);
    98	    if (!isRecord(pipelines)) continue;
    99	    const rebuiltPipelines: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   100	    for (const eventKey of Object.keys(pipelines)) {
   101	      const pipeline = ownGet(pipelines, eventKey);
   102	      if (!Array.isArray(pipeline)) continue;
   103	      const base = `${ownerPath === "$" ? "" : `${(ownerPath ?? "").replace("$.", "")}.`}${ownerKey}.${inner}.${eventKey}`;
   104	      defineOwn(
   105	        rebuiltPipelines,
   106	        eventKey,
   107	        pipeline.map((binding: unknown, index: number) => {
   108	          const carried: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   109	          if (isRecord(binding)) {
   110	            for (const field of hook.carry) defineOwn(carried, field, ownGet(binding, field));
   111	          }
   112	          defineOwn(carried, hook.into, effectiveConfigs.get(`${base}[${String(index)}]`));
   113	          return carried;
   114	        }),
   115	      );
   116	    }
   117	    defineOwn(owner, inner, rebuiltPipelines);
   118	  }
   119	}
   120	
   121	/**
   122	 * Run every declared normalizer hook over a VALIDATED value. The caller
   123	 * guarantees the value carries no findings — a declaration that refused
   124	 * the input never reaches derivation.
   125	 */
   126	export function normalize(
   127	  surface: SurfaceDecl,
   128	  value: unknown,
   129	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
   130	): unknown {
   131	  if (!isRecord(value)) return value;
   132	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) {
   133	    if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   134	      expandFlagMaps(value, hook);
   135	      continue;
   136	    }
   137	    materializeConfigs(value, hook, effectiveConfigs);
   138	  }
   139	  return value;
   140	}

   246	/** The position a child of `at` occupies, or undefined where the enclosing
   247	 * position is itself unaddressable (inside a value-class DEFINITION, which
   248	 * the engine only ever evaluates at a reference site). */
   249	function childAt(ctx: Context, at: DeclCursor | undefined, step: Parameters<typeof descend>[2]): DeclCursor | undefined {
   250	  return at === undefined ? undefined : descend(ctx.surface, at, step);
