   282	/** vocabulary #19 — uninterpreted pass-through. */
   283	export interface RawDecl extends NodeBase {
   284	  readonly kind: "raw";
   285	  /** A raw node may still assert a container kind (ch12-C3's `config`). */
   286	  readonly containerMessage?: MessageTemplate;
   287	}
   288	
   289	/** `map.plain + canonicalJsonSafe` — the agent-config value class
   290	 * (ch12-C7). A PLAIN string-keyed map (never a class instance, never a
   291	 * JS Map); a non-plain container SUPPRESSES the canonical lane. */
   292	export interface MapPlainDecl extends NodeBase {
   293	  readonly kind: "map.plain";
   294	  readonly containerMessage: MessageTemplate;
   295	  readonly canonicalJsonSafe: { readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   296	}
   297	
   298	/** vocabulary #15 — a reusable named value class, with the citing site's
   299	 * `{label}`. */
   300	export interface ValueClassRefDecl extends NodeBase {
   301	  readonly kind: "valueClass";
   302	  readonly valueClass: string;
   303	  readonly label?: string;
   304	}
   305	
   306	/** vocabulary #16 — hand-off to an injected registration's own
   307	 * declaration. */
   308	export interface DelegateDecl extends NodeBase {
   309	  readonly kind: "delegate";
   310	  readonly registry: "gateCatalog";
   311	  /** The sibling field whose value names the registration. */
   312	  readonly by: string;
   313	  /** The delegation contract's own belt: a registration that reports
   314	   * failure with ZERO findings must still block admission, or the binding
   315	   * would be admitted with no effective config. A property of the
   316	   * `delegate` construct, not of any one row (audit flag I3). */
   317	  readonly beltMessage: MessageTemplate;
   318	  /** vocabulary #13 — the resolution lane this hand-off presupposes. */
   319	  readonly dependsOn?: readonly string[];
   320	}
   321	
   322	export type NodeDecl =
   323	  | MapFixedDecl
   324	  | MapOpenDecl
   325	  | MapPlainDecl
   326	  | ListDecl
   327	  | StringDecl
   328	  | IntegerDecl
   329	  | EnumDecl
   330	  | UnionDecl
   331	  | RawDecl
   332	  | ValueClassRefDecl
   333	  | DelegateDecl;
   334	
   335	/**
   336	 * vocabulary #12's `equals` relation — a two-direction set equality with
   337	 * its own message per direction and its own path grain per direction
   338	 * (ch8-C16's measured form: the used-but-undeclared finding reports at
   339	 * the container, the declared-but-unused finding at the entry).
   340	 */
   341	export interface EqualsRuleDecl {
   342	  readonly tag: string;
   343	  readonly rows: readonly string[];
   344	  readonly relation: "equals";
   345	  readonly left: Selector;
   346	  readonly right: Selector;
   347	  readonly missingFromLeft: { readonly at: FindingPathTemplate; readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   348	  readonly missingFromRight: { readonly at: FindingPathTemplate; readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   349	  readonly dependsOn?: readonly string[];
   350	}
   351	
   352	/**
   353	 * D3 — the NORMALIZER's declared hooks. A declaration says what is legal;
   354	 * it does not compute a value, so derivation is named here and realized in
   355	 * `normalizer.ts`, never in the validator. Each hook is a named capability
   356	 * with its ratified rows; adding one is D7 format growth, not an edit.
   357	 */
   358	export type NormalizerHookDecl =
   359	  | {
   360	      readonly tag: string;
   361	      readonly rows: readonly string[];
   362	      readonly hook: "expandAdvancesRound";
   363	      /** The open map of entries the flag map is expanded per. */
   364	      readonly over: NormalizerPath;
   365	      /** The entry's edge map, relative to the entry. */
   366	      readonly edges: string;
   367	      /** The declared advancing set. */
   368	      readonly advanceSet: NormalizerPath;
   369	      /** The produced sibling field. */
   370	      readonly into: string;
   371	    }
   372	  | {
   373	      readonly tag: string;
   374	      readonly rows: readonly string[];
   375	      readonly hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs";
   376	      /** The open map of pipelines whose members carry a delegated config. */
   377	      readonly over: NormalizerPath;
   378	      /** The binding fields carried into the admitted form verbatim. */
   379	      readonly carry: readonly string[];
   380	      /** The produced field the effective config lands in. */
   381	      readonly into: string;
   382	    };
   383	
   384	/**
   385	 * The SUBSTRATE block (audit §2.3): how bytes become a value graph. Its
   386	 * obligations are declared here as data; the yaml library realizes them,
   387	 * which is why their WORDING is residual R6 (library-owned) rather than
   388	 * engine parity.
   389	 */
   390	export interface SubstrateDecl {
   391	  /** ch8-C6: the read stage's decode wording. */
   392	  readonly read: SubstrateBranch & { readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   393	  readonly parse: {
   394	    /** ch8-C34: the only accepted `%YAML` directive version, and the
   395	     * finding a different one produces (`{key}` is the authored one). */
   396	    readonly directive: SubstrateBranch & {
   397	      readonly only: string;
   398	      readonly message: MessageTemplate;
   399	    };
   400	    /** ch8-C4: the duplicate-key wording. */
   401	    readonly duplicateKeys: SubstrateBranch & { readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   402	  };
   403	  /** ch8-C5: the resolved graph must be acyclic, and the finding it
   404	   * yields at the cycle's path. */
   405	  readonly resolve: { readonly graph: SubstrateBranch & { readonly message: MessageTemplate } };
   406	  /** ch8-C23: the CLOSED issue-code namespace. Every `code` any node in
   407	   * the surface declares must be a member — checked at declaration load. */
   408	  readonly codes: SubstrateBranch & { readonly values: readonly string[] };
   409	  /** ch8-C22/C36: the every-stage belt's own finding. */
   410	  readonly internalFailure: SubstrateBranch & {
   411	    readonly path: string;
   412	    readonly message: MessageTemplate;
   413	  };
   414	}
   415	
   416	/**
   417	 * Every substrate branch is TAG-ADDRESSABLE, for the same reason every
   418	 * node is (design review F6): D4's contract form is "decisions plus
   419	 * declaration pointers", and a branch with no tag is an authority a
   420	 * ratified row can only cite by repeating its text — which is the one
   421	 * thing D4 forbids.
   422	 */
   423	export interface SubstrateBranch {
   424	  readonly tag: string;
   425	  readonly rows: readonly string[];
   426	}
   427	
   428	/** One declared surface: substrate + node tree + the named residual's
   429	 * declarable halves. */
   430	export interface SurfaceDecl {
   431	  readonly substrate: SubstrateDecl;
   432	  readonly root: NodeDecl;
   433	  readonly valueClasses: Readonly<Record<string, NodeDecl>>;
   434	  readonly crossRules: readonly EqualsRuleDecl[];
   435	  readonly normalizers: readonly NormalizerHookDecl[];
   436	}
   850	  interface Guard {
   851	    readonly claim: string;
   852	    readonly make: (ok: boolean) => SurfaceDecl;
   853	    readonly problem: RegExp;
   854	  }
   855	
   856	  const surfaceOf = (over: Partial<SurfaceDecl>, root: Record<string, unknown>): SurfaceDecl =>
   857	    ({
   858	      substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
   859	      valueClasses: {
   860	        idClass: { kind: "string", tag: "vc", rows: ["x"], grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad" } },
   861	      },
   862	      crossRules: [],
   863	      normalizers: [],
   864	      root: { kind: "map.fixed", tag: "root", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", unknownMessage: "u", ...root },
   865	      ...over,
   866	    }) as SurfaceDecl;
   867	
   868	  const plainRoot = { fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], valueClass: "idClass" } } };
   869	  const base = (over: Partial<SurfaceDecl> = {}, root: Record<string, unknown> = plainRoot): SurfaceDecl =>
   870	    surfaceOf(over, root);
   871	
   872	  /** A root with an open map `names` plus a scalar that selects over it. */
   873	  const selecting = (relation: "keysOf" | "valuesOf" | "collect", path: string): SurfaceDecl =>
   874	    base({}, {
   875	      fields: {
   876	        names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "n", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container",
   877	          entry: { kind: "string", tag: "ne", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },
   878	        pick: { kind: "string", tag: "p", rows: ["x"],
   879	          memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { [relation]: path } as never, message: "m" } },
   880	      },
   881	    });
   882	
   883	  const hookSurface = (over: Partial<Record<"over" | "edges" | "into" | "advanceSet", string>>): SurfaceDecl =>
   884	    base({
   885	      normalizers: [{
   886	        tag: "n-h", rows: ["x"], hook: "expandAdvancesRound",
   887	        over: over.over ?? "$.steps", edges: over.edges ?? "transitions",
   888	        advanceSet: over.advanceSet ?? "$.adv", into: over.into ?? "flags",
   889	      }],
   890	    }, {
   891	      fields: {
   892	        adv: { kind: "list", tag: "adv", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", memberLaneAt: "index",
   893	          member: { kind: "string", tag: "advm", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },
   894	        steps: { kind: "map.open", tag: "st", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container",
   895	          entry: { kind: "map.fixed", tag: "ste", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", unknownMessage: "u",
   896	            fields: {
   897	              transitions: { kind: "map.open", tag: "tr", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container",
   898	                entry: { kind: "string", tag: "trv", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },
   899	              flags: { kind: "raw", tag: "fl", rows: ["x"], channel: "direct" },
   900	            } } },
   901	      },
   902	    });
   903	
   904	  /** Two structurally identical open maps under one fixed map, one of whose
   905	   * field names CONTAINS a dot. The hook names one or the other, so the
   906	   * broken and corrected forms differ in exactly that one reference. */
   907	  const dottedHook = (ok: boolean): SurfaceDecl => {
   908	    const openMap = (tag: string): Record<string, unknown> => ({
   909	      kind: "map.open", tag, rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container",
   910	      entry: { kind: "map.fixed", tag: `${tag}e`, rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", unknownMessage: "u",
   911	        fields: {
   912	          transitions: { kind: "map.open", tag: `${tag}t`, rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c",
   913	            keyLaneAt: "container", entry: { kind: "string", tag: `${tag}v`, rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },
   914	          flags: { kind: "raw", tag: `${tag}f`, rows: ["x"] },
   915	        } },
   916	    });
   917	    return base({
   918	      normalizers: [{
   919	        tag: "n-h", rows: ["x"], hook: "expandAdvancesRound",
   920	        over: ok ? "$.dotted.plain" : "$.dotted.a.b",
   921	        edges: "transitions", advanceSet: "$.adv", into: "flags",
   922	      }],
   923	    }, {
   924	      fields: {
   925	        adv: { kind: "list", tag: "adv", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", memberLaneAt: "index",
   926	          member: { kind: "string", tag: "advm", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },
   927	        dotted: { kind: "map.fixed", tag: "d", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", unknownMessage: "u",
   928	          fields: { "a.b": openMap("ab"), plain: openMap("pl") } },
   929	      },
   930	    });
   931	  };
   932	
   933	  const binding = (by: string): SurfaceDecl =>
   934	    base({}, {
   935	      fields: {
   936	        uses: { kind: "string", tag: "u", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
   937	        config: { kind: "delegate", tag: "cfg", rows: ["x"], registry: "gateCatalog", by,
   938	          beltMessage: "b", dependsOn: ["u"] },
   939	      },
   940	    });
   941	
   942	  const GUARDS: readonly Guard[] = [
   943	    { claim: "an unknown value-class name — the measured shame case",
   944	      make: (ok) => base({}, { fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], valueClass: ok ? "idClass" : "TYPO" } } }),
   945	      problem: /value class "TYPO" is not declared/u },
   946	    { claim: "a selector path in the documented-but-inert `..` form",
   947	      make: (ok) => selecting("keysOf", ok ? "$.names" : "..names"),
   948	      problem: /selector path "\.\.names" must start with "\$"/u },
   949	    { claim: "a selector path with an empty segment",
   950	      make: (ok) => selecting("keysOf", ok ? "$.names" : "$..names"),
   951	      problem: /has an empty segment/u },
   952	    { claim: "a selector path with `*` inside a segment",
   953	      make: (ok) => selecting("keysOf", ok ? "$.names" : "$.na*mes"),
   954	      problem: /"\*" is legal only as a whole segment/u },
   955	    { claim: "a selector path that is well-formed but addresses NO declared position",
   956	      make: (ok) => selecting("keysOf", ok ? "$.names" : "$.nmaes"),
   957	      problem: /addresses "\$\.nmaes", which the declaration does not declare/u },
   958	    { claim: "a selector whose target exists but is the WRONG KIND for the relation",
   959	      make: (ok) => selecting(ok ? "keysOf" : "valuesOf", "$.names"),
   960	      problem: /valuesOf\("\$\.names"\) addresses a map\.open whose value can be map; valuesOf reads only list/u },
   961	    { claim: "a delegate reading a sibling field the enclosing map does not declare",
   962	      make: (ok) => binding(ok ? "uses" : "ues"),
   963	      problem: /delegate reads sibling field "ues"/u },
   964	    { claim: "a channel mark where the engine does not read one",
   965	      make: (ok) => base({}, { fields: { list: { kind: "list", tag: "l", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", memberLaneAt: "index",
   966	        member: { kind: "string", tag: "lm", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t", ...(ok ? {} : { channel: "file" }) } } } }),
   967	      problem: /carries channel "file", which is read only on a field of a map\.fixed/u },
   968	    { claim: "a dependsOn naming a tag nothing declares",
   969	      make: (ok) => base({}, {
   970	        fields: {
   971	          uses: { kind: "string", tag: "u", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
   972	          config: { kind: "delegate", tag: "c", rows: ["x"], registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses",
   973	            beltMessage: "b", dependsOn: [ok ? "u" : "no-such-tag"] },
   974	        } }),
   975	      problem: /dependsOn names "no-such-tag", which is not a declared tag/u },
   976	    { claim: "a dependsOn naming a declared tag the engine never gives a status",
   977	      make: (ok) => base({}, {
   978	        fields: {
   979	          uses: { kind: "string", tag: "u", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
   980	          config: { kind: "delegate", tag: "c", rows: ["x"], registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses",
   981	            beltMessage: "b", dependsOn: [ok ? "u" : "d-read"] },
   982	        } }),
   983	      problem: /is declared but is not a node the engine records a status for/u },
   984	    { claim: "a dependsOn INSIDE a value-class definition",
   985	      make: (ok) => base({
   986	        valueClasses: {
   987	          idClass: { kind: "string", tag: "vc", rows: ["x"], grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad" },
   988	            memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "m",
   989	              dependsOn: [ok ? "n" : "ghost-tag"] } },
   990	        },
   991	      }, {
   992	        fields: {
   993	          names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "n", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container",
   994	            entry: { kind: "string", tag: "ne", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },
   995	          id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], valueClass: "idClass" },
   996	        } }),
   997	      problem: /dependsOn names "ghost-tag", which is not a declared tag/u },
   998	    { claim: "a tag declared twice",
   999	      make: (ok) => base({}, { fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: ok ? "id" : "root", rows: ["x"], valueClass: "idClass" } } }),
  1000	      problem: /tag "root" is declared more than once/u },
  1001	    { claim: "a node citing no ratified row",
  1002	      make: (ok) => base({}, { fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ok ? ["x"] : [], valueClass: "idClass" } } }),
  1003	      problem: /cites no ratified row/u },
  1004	    { claim: "an issue code outside the closed namespace",
  1005	      make: (ok) => base({}, { fields: { id: { kind: "enum", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], members: [{ value: "a" }], message: "m",
  1006	        code: ok ? "gate_evaluator_unavailable" : "made_up_code" } } }),
  1007	      problem: /issue code "made_up_code" is outside the declared namespace/u },
  1008	    { claim: "a message using a placeholder the engine does not supply",
  1009	      make: (ok) => base({}, { ...plainRoot, unknownMessage: ok ? "unknown {key}" : "unknown {notASlot}" }),
  1010	      problem: /unknown placeholder \{notASlot\}/u },
  1011	    { claim: "a SUBSTRATE message using a placeholder the engine does not supply",
  1012	      make: (ok) => ({
  1013	        ...base(),
  1014	        substrate: {
  1015	          ...templateFormat.substrate,
  1016	          read: { ...templateFormat.substrate.read, message: ok ? "bad bytes" : "bad bytes {nope}" },
  1017	        },
  1018	      }),
  1019	      problem: /substrate\.read: unknown placeholder \{nope\}/u },
  1020	    { claim: "a finding-path template written in selector syntax",
  1021	      make: (ok) => base({
  1022	        crossRules: [{ tag: "eq", rows: ["x"], relation: "equals",
  1023	          left: { keysOf: "$.names" }, right: { keysOf: "$.names" },
  1024	          missingFromLeft: { at: ok ? "names" : "$.names", message: "m" },
  1025	          missingFromRight: { at: "names", message: "m" } }],
  1026	      }, {
  1027	        fields: { names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "n", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container",
  1028	          entry: { kind: "string", tag: "ne", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } } },
  1029	      }),
  1030	      problem: /uses selector syntax; a finding path is rendered, not walked/u },
  1031	    { claim: "a normalizer operand path that is not document-rooted",
  1032	      make: (ok) => hookSurface({ over: ok ? "$.steps" : "steps" }),
  1033	      problem: /normalizer path "steps" must start with "\$"/u },
  1034	    { claim: "a normalizer operand path with `*` inside a segment",
  1035	      make: (ok) => hookSurface({ over: ok ? "$.steps" : "$.ste*ps" }),
  1036	      problem: /normalizer path "\$\.ste\*ps" uses "\*" inside a segment/u },
  1037	    { claim: "a normalizer operand path addressing NO declared position",
  1038	      make: (ok) => hookSurface({ over: ok ? "$.steps" : "$.setps" }),
  1039	      problem: /over: "\$\.setps" is not a declared position/u },
  1040	    { claim: "a normalizer reading an entry field the entry does not declare",
  1041	      make: (ok) => hookSurface({ edges: ok ? "transitions" : "trasitions" }),
  1042	      problem: /edges: "trasitions" is not a field of "\$\.steps\.\*"/u },
  1043	    { claim: "a normalizer WRITING a field the entry does not declare",
  1044	      make: (ok) => hookSurface({ into: ok ? "flags" : "flgas" }),
  1045	      problem: /into: "flgas" is not a field of "\$\.steps\.\*"/u },
  1046	    // --- the four SILENT cases: the gate said "closed" over an inert rule.
  1047	    { claim: "collect over a target whose value can be a non-string — readable for no input",
  1048	      make: (ok) => base({}, {
  1049	        fields: {
  1050	          count: { kind: "integer", tag: "cnt", rows: ["x"], resolvedForm: { safeInteger: true, min: 1, message: "m" } },
  1051	          label: { kind: "string", tag: "lbl", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
  1052	          pick: { kind: "string", tag: "p", rows: ["x"],
  1053	            memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { collect: ok ? "$.label" : "$.count" }, message: "m" } },
  1054	        } }),
  1055	      problem: /collect\("\$\.count"\) addresses a integer whose value can be other; collect reads only string/u },
  1056	    { claim: "keysOf over a UNION that can also hold a string — readable for only some inputs",
  1057	      make: (ok) => base({}, {
  1058	        fields: {
  1059	          names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "n", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container",
  1060	            entry: { kind: "string", tag: "ne", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },
  1061	          rt: { kind: "union", tag: "rt", rows: ["x"], literals: ["none"], message: "m",
  1062	            mapCase: { kind: "map.fixed", tag: "rtm", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", unknownMessage: "u",
  1063	              fields: { kind: { kind: "string", tag: "rtk", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } } } },
  1064	          pick: { kind: "string", tag: "p", rows: ["x"],
  1065	            memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: ok ? "$.names" : "$.rt" }, message: "m" } },
  1066	        } }),
  1067	      problem: /keysOf\("\$\.rt"\) addresses a union whose value can be map \| string/u },
  1068	    { claim: "a selector naming the engine-INTERNAL list-member address",
  1069	      make: (ok) => base({}, {
  1070	        fields: {
  1071	          items: { kind: "list", tag: "it", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", memberLaneAt: "index",
  1072	            member: { kind: "string", tag: "itm", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },
  1073	          pick: { kind: "string", tag: "p", rows: ["x"],
  1074	            memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { valuesOf: ok ? "$.items" : "$.items[]" }, message: "m" } },
  1075	        } }),
  1076	      problem: /addresses "\$\.items\[\]", which the declaration does not declare/u },
  1077	    { claim: "a selector path through a declared field name that CONTAINS a dot",
  1078	      make: (ok) => base({}, {
  1079	        fields: {
  1080	          dotted: { kind: "map.fixed", tag: "d", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", unknownMessage: "u",
  1081	            fields: {
  1082	              "a.b": { kind: "string", tag: "ab", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
  1083	              plain: { kind: "string", tag: "pl", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
  1084	            } },
  1085	          pick: { kind: "string", tag: "p", rows: ["x"],
  1086	            memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { collect: ok ? "$.dotted.plain" : "$.dotted.a.b" }, message: "m" } },
  1087	        } }),
  1088	      problem: /addresses "\$\.dotted\.a\.b", which the declaration does not declare/u },
  1089	    // --- the four the ROUND AFTER those four found, and which the single
  1090	    // --- resolver closes by construction rather than one patch each.
  1091	    { claim: "a selector reading a target the engine visits on ONE channel only",
  1092	      make: (ok) => base({}, {
  1093	        fields: {
  1094	          names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "n", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container",
  1095	            entry: { kind: "string", tag: "ne", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
  1096	            ...(ok ? {} : { channel: "direct" }) },
  1097	          pick: { kind: "string", tag: "p", rows: ["x"],
  1098	            memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "m" } },
  1099	        } }),
  1100	      problem: /passes through "\$\.names", which is declared channel "direct"/u },
  1101	    { claim: "a selector reading `*` at a FIXED map, where the walk records no wildcard position",
  1102	      make: (ok) => base({}, {
  1103	        fields: {
  1104	          m: { kind: "map.fixed", tag: "m", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", unknownMessage: "u",
  1105	            fields: {
  1106	              "*": { kind: "string", tag: "star", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
  1107	              plain: { kind: "string", tag: "pl", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
  1108	            } },
  1109	          pick: { kind: "string", tag: "p", rows: ["x"],
  1110	            memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { collect: ok ? "$.m.plain" : "$.m.*" }, message: "m" } },
  1111	        } }),
  1112	      problem: /addresses "\$\.m\.\*", which the declaration does not declare/u },
  1113	    { claim: "a delegate reading a sibling field whose value is not a string",
  1114	      make: (ok) => base({}, {
  1115	        fields: {
  1116	          uses: { kind: "string", tag: "u", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
  1117	          count: { kind: "integer", tag: "cnt", rows: ["x"], resolvedForm: { safeInteger: true, min: 1, message: "m" } },
  1118	          config: { kind: "delegate", tag: "cfg", rows: ["x"], registry: "gateCatalog", by: ok ? "uses" : "count",
  1119	            beltMessage: "b", dependsOn: ["u"] },
  1120	        } }),
  1121	      problem: /delegate reads sibling field "count", whose value can be other; the registration is looked up by a string/u },
  1122	    { claim: "a `^` selector on a LIST MEMBER, whose container is an array the walk cannot read",
  1123	      make: (ok) => base({}, {
  1124	        fields: {
  1125	          names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "n", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container",
  1126	            entry: { kind: "string", tag: "ne", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },
  1127	          items: { kind: "list", tag: "it", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", memberLaneAt: "index",
  1128	            member: { kind: "string", tag: "itm", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t",

codex
A resolver-kettőződést eddig nem látok: a deklarációs címképzés, a gate és a runtime selector ugyanazt a `descend`/`resolveSelectorPath` modellt használja. Viszont igazolás alatt van egy szomszédos runtime-skip: egy deklarált, de opcionálisan hiányzó selector-cél nem kap `completed` állapotot, ezért a rá váró szabály a deferred queue második körében is pending maradhat.

codex
### F1 — Opcionálisan hiányzó selector-cél mellett a szabály végleg pending marad

CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: B  
WHAT: A gate zártnak fogad el egy selector-referenciát egy opcionális fix-map mezőre, de ha ez a mező jogszerűen hiányzik, a motor nem értékeli és nem jelöli completednek a pozíciót; a membership-szabály a deferred queue újrapróbálásakor is pending marad, majd finding nélkül eldobódik.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/defineSurface.ts:202`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:583`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:898`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:1323`  
EVIDENCE:

```text
pnpm exec tsx -e 'import { closureProblems } from "./v3/src/definition/schema/defineSurface.ts"; import { runSurface } from "./v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts"; import { templateFormat } from "./v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts"; const surface = { substrate: templateFormat.substrate, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], root: { kind: "map.fixed", tag: "qa-root", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "container", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields: { names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "qa-names", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "container", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: { kind: "string", tag: "qa-name", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "type" } }, pick: { kind: "string", tag: "qa-pick", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "type", memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "NOT_A_NAME" } } } } }; console.log("closure=" + JSON.stringify(closureProblems(surface))); for (const input of [{ pick: "ghost" }, { names: {}, pick: "ghost" }, { names: { ok: "v" }, pick: "ghost" }]) console.log(JSON.stringify(input) + " => " + JSON.stringify(runSurface(surface, input, { channel: { kind: "direct" } }).findings));'
closure=[]
{"pick":"ghost"} => []
{"names":{},"pick":"ghost"} => [{"path":"pick","message":"NOT_A_NAME"}]
{"names":{"ok":"v"},"pick":"ghost"} => [{"path":"pick","message":"NOT_A_NAME"}]
```

WHY IT MATTERS: A maintainer a `closure=[]` eredményből tévesen arra következtet, hogy a deklarált membership-szabály minden jogszerű inputon eldől; valójában pusztán a célmező hiánya kikapcsolja.  
FIX SKETCH: A gate utasítsa vissza azt a selector-utat, amely fix-map opcionális mezőn halad át és így jogszerű inputon nem feltétlenül kerül értékelésre.
